## Corporate Customers

### byExternalidAddressesByEntityexternalidEdit.post

`corporateCustomers.byExternalidAddressesByEntityexternalidEdit.post`

Editing the address of the corporate customer

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.simlacom.api.corporateCustomers.byExternalidAddressesByEntityexternalidEdit.post({
	path: { externalId: 'demo', entityExternalId: 'demo' },
	body: {
		address: { index: 'demo' },
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  externalId: string,
  entityExternalId: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  by?: custom,
  site?: string,
  entityBy?: custom
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  address?: {
    index?: string | null,
    countryIso?: string | null,
    region?: string | null,
    regionId?: number | null,
    city?: string | null,
    cityId?: number | null,
    cityType?: string,
    street?: string | null,
    streetId?: number | null,
    streetType?: string,
    building?: string | null,
    flat?: string | null,
    floor?: number | null,
    block?: number | null,
    house?: string | null,
    housing?: string,
    metro?: string | null,
    notes?: string | null,
    text?: string,
    isMain?: boolean,
    externalId?: string,
    name?: string
  }
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | No | Query result (successful/unsuccessful) |
| `id` | `number` | No | — |



---

### byExternalidEdit.post

`corporateCustomers.byExternalidEdit.post`

Corporate customer editing

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.simlacom.api.corporateCustomers.byExternalidEdit.post({
	path: { externalId: 'demo' },
	body: {
		customerCorporate: { externalId: 'demo' },
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  externalId: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  by?: custom,
  site?: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  customerCorporate?: {
    externalId?: string,
    createdAt?: string,
    vip?: boolean,
    bad?: boolean,
    customFields?: {
    },
    personalDiscount?: number,
    discountCardNumber?: string | null,
    nickName?: string,
    managerId?: number
  }
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | No | Query result (successful/unsuccessful) |
| `id` | `number` | No | Customer internal ID |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | Customer customer (not returned by default) |

### byExternalidPlatesByPlateidPrint.get

`orders.byExternalidPlatesByPlateidPrint.get`

Getting the file of the printed form for the order

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.simlacom.api.orders.byExternalidPlatesByPlateidPrint.get({
	path: { externalId: 'demo', plateId: 1 },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `never` | No | — |

The OpenAPI reference documents success/error statuses but no response content schema. The operation validates a JSON object; its exact fields are unverified.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  externalId: string,
  plateId: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  by?: custom,
  site?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `Record<string, custom>`

### fixExternalIds.post

`orders.fixExternalIds.post`

The mass recording of orders external ID

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.simlacom.api.orders.fixExternalIds.post({
	body: {
		orders: [],
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | No | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
```

### history.get

`orders.history.get`

Getting the order change history

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.simlacom.api.orders.history.get({
	query: { filter[orderId]: 0, filter[sinceId]: 0 },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | No | — |
| `query` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `never` | No | — |

<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
  limit?: custom,
  page?: number,
  filter[
    startDate
  ]?: string,
  filter[
    endDate
  ]?: string,
  filter[
    orderId
  ]: number,
  filter[
    orderExternalId
  ]?: string,
  filter[
    sinceId

}
```

### linksCreate.post

`orders.linksCreate.post`

Creating a link between orders

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.simlacom.api.orders.linksCreate.post({
	body: {
		link: { comment: 'demo' },
	},
});
```

## Orders Picking

### getPacksById.get

`ordersPicking.getPacksById.get`

Getting information on pack

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.simlacom.api.ordersPicking.getPacksById.get({
	path: { id: 1 },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `never` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | No | Query result (successful/unsuccessful) |
| `pack` | `object` | No | Pack - items pack, within the one item position, from one warehouse |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="pack full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null,
  purchasePrice?: number | null,
  quantity?: number | null,
  unit?: {
    code?: string,
    name?: string,
    sym?: string
  },
  store?: string,

    externalId?: string,
    id?: number | null,
    externalIds?: {
    }[],

      id?: number
    },
    offer?: {
      externalId?: string,
      xmlId?: string
    }
  },

  deliveryNoteNumber?: string
}
```
</Accordion>

### packs.get

`ordersPicking.packs.get`

```ts
await corsair.simlacom.api.ordersPicking.packs.get({});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | No | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |

```ts
{
}
```

```ts
{
```
